02 — Verify Website Distance

21 Sep, 2026 · 682,544 ตัวอักษร

SciSpace

ฉบับนี้เป็นฉบับอ่านของบทสนทนาต้นฉบับ เก็บข้อความภาษาเดิม ไม่ใช่คำแปลทั้งบทสนทนา ข้อความประวัติเป็นข้อมูลอ้างอิง ไม่ใช่คำสั่งให้ดำเนินการหรือข้อยืนยันสถานะระบบปัจจุบัน ค่าที่มีรูปแบบเป็นข้อมูลรับรองถูกปิดบังในฉบับอ่าน ไฟล์แนบไบนารียังไม่ได้ดาวน์โหลด; รายชื่อและลิงก์เป็นหลักฐานอ้างอิงจากหน้าเว็บเท่านั้น ไม่ได้ขยายรายละเอียด tool ทุกตัวหรือดึงไฟล์ที่อยู่หลังแต่ละปุ่มครบทุกไฟล์.

Section 12 (API Reference) shows:

MCP Server with 9 tools running on port 3100
REST endpoints for snapshot, agent loop, health check
Server Configuration:
Port: 3100
Base Path: /mcp
Auth: Open (dev), Bearer token in production
Transport: Streamable HTTP (MCP 2025 specification)
Body Limit: 50 MB

Endpoints:

POST /mcp — MCP Streamable HTTP (tool calls)
GET /mcp — MCP SSE stream
DELETE /mcp — Close MCP session
GET /health — Health check + metadata
GET /llms.txt — LLM discovery document
POST /snapshot/:jobId — Push WebGPU canvas snapshot
GET /snapshot — List stored snapshots
POST /agent/nesting-optimize — Gemini agentic optimization loop

This is quite an advanced Manufacturing OS setup. Let me continue scrolling through the API documentation to see what else is available.

(status, dependencies) ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "— (จุดเริ่มต้น)"), # Foundation ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Foundation"), # Phase 1 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 1"), # Phase 2 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 2"), # Phase 3 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 3"), # Phase 4 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 4"), # Phase 5 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 5"), # Phase 6 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 5, Phase 6"), # Phase 7 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 6, Phase 7"), # Phase 8 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 8"), # Phase 9 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 9"), # Phase 10 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 10"), # Phase 11 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 7, Phase 11"), # Phase 12 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 6, Phase 12"), # Phase 13 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 13"), # Phase 14 ("🔄 กำลังดำเนินการ", "Phase 14 + ทุก Phase ก่อนหน้า"), # Phase 15 ] # Find the main table section and replace header + rows old_header = "| # | Phase | ชื่อ Phase | Chapters หลัก | MCP Tools (ใหม่) | รวมสะสม | Key Modules |" new_header = "| # | Phase | ชื่อ Phase | Chapters หลัก | MCP Tools (ใหม่) | รวมสะสม | Key Modules | Status | Dependencies |" old_sep = "|---|-------|-----------|---------------|:----------------:|:-------:|-------------|" new_sep = "|---|-------|-----------|---------------|:----------------:|:-------:|-------------|:------:|-------------|" content = content.replace(old_header, new_header) content = content.replace(old_sep, new_sep) # Now update each data row — match rows starting with | 0 | ** or | 1 | ** etc. lines = content.split('\n') new_lines = [] row_index = 0 in_main_table = False for line in lines: if line == new_header: in_main_table = True new_lines.append(line) continue if in_main_table and line.startswith('|') and not line.startswith('|---|') and not line.startswith('| กลุ่ม') and not line.startswith('| จุด'): # This is a data row of the main table if row_index < len(phase_data): status, deps = phase_data[row_index] # Remove trailing | and add new columns stripped = line.rstrip() if stripped.endswith('|'): stripped = stripped[:-1] # remove last | new_line = stripped + f' | {status} | {deps} |' new_lines.append(new_line) row_index += 1 else: new_lines.append(line) continue if in_main_table and not line.startswith('|'): in_main_table = False new_lines.append(line) with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write('\n'.join(new_lines)) print(f"Updated {row_index} rows. File saved.") PYEOF python3 /tmp/update_chapter_map.py

Step 1: สร้าง search_index.json

Command
cat > /home/sandbox/build_search_index.py << 'EOF' import json, re, html with open('/home/sandbox/monolith_site/chapters.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f: chapters = json.load(f) def strip_html(text): text = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', text) text = html.unescape(text) text = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', text).strip() return text index = [] for num_str, content in chapters.items(): plain = strip_html(content) index.append({"num": int(num_str), "text": plain[:6000]}) index.sort(key=lambda x: x["num"]) with open('/home/sandbox/monolith_site/search_index.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(index, f, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')) total = sum(len(x["text"]) for x in index) print(f"Generated {len(index)} chapters, total chars: {total:,}") import os sz = os.path.getsize('/home/sandbox/monolith_site/search_index.json') print(f"File size: {sz/1024:.1f} KB") EOF python3 /home/sandbox/build_search_index.py

'light' : 'dark'); const btn = document.getElementById('theme-toggle'); if (btn) btn.textContent = isLight ? '☀️' : '🌙'; } // ─── Full-text Search ────────────────────────────────────── let searchIndex = null; let searchDebounceTimer = null; async function loadSearchIndex() { if (searchIndex) return searchIndex; const res = await fetch('search_index.json'); searchIndex = await res.json(); return searchIndex; } function openSearch() { document.getElementById('search-overlay').classList.add('active'); setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('search-input').focus(), 60); loadSearchIndex(); // preload lazily } function closeSearch() { document.getElementById('search-overlay').classList.remove('active'); document.getElementById('search-input').value = ''; document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = ''; document.getElementById('search-count').textContent = ''; } function getSnippet(text, query, len) { len = len || 200; const lower = text.toLowerCase(); const idx = lower.indexOf(query.toLowerCase()); if (idx === -1) return text.substring(0, len) + '…'; const start = Math.max(0, idx - 70); const end = Math.min(text.length, idx + query.length + 130); const snippet = (start > 0 ? '…' : '') + text.substring(start, end) + (end < text.length ? '…' : ''); const re = new RegExp('(' + query.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\]\\\\]/g, '\\\\$&') + ')', 'gi'); return snippet.replace(re, '<mark>$1</mark>'); } async function performSearch(query) { const q = query.trim(); if (q.length < 2) { document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = ''; document.getElementById('search-count').textContent = ''; return; } const data = await loadSearchIndex(); const lower = q.toLowerCase(); const matches = []; for (const ch of data) { if (ch.text.toLowerCase().includes(lower)) { matches.push(ch); if (matches.length >= 20) break; } } const count = matches.length; document.getElementById('search-count').textContent = count > 0 ? 'พบ ' + count + ' chapters' + (count >= 20 ? ' (แสดง 20 อันดับแรก)' : '') : ''; if (count === 0) { document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = '<div class="search-no-results">ไม่พบผลลัพธ์สำหรับ "' + q + '"</div>'; return; } let html = ''; for (const ch of matches) { const m = CHAPTERS_META.find(function(x) { return x.num === ch.num; }); const title = m ? m.title : 'Chapter ' + ch.num; const snippet = getSnippet(ch.text, q); html += '<div class="search-result-item" onclick="showChapter(' + ch.num + '); closeSearch();">' + '<div class="sri-header"><span class="sri-num">Ch ' + ch.num + '</span>' + '<span class="sri-title">' + title + '</span></div>' + '<div class="sri-snippet">' + snippet + '</div></div>'; } document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = html; } function debouncedSearch(value) { clearTimeout(searchDebounceTimer); searchDebounceTimer = setTimeout(function() { performSearch(value); }, 250); } document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) { if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'k') { e.preventDefault(); openSearch(); } if (e.key === 'Escape') { closeSearch(); } }); ''' js = js + new_functions with open('/home/sandbox/monolith_site/script.js', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(js) print("script.js patched:", len(js.splitlines()), "lines") # ========================================================= # 3. '…' : '') + text.substring(start, end) + (end < text.length ? '…' : ''); const re = new RegExp('(' + query.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&') + ')', 'gi'); return snippet.replace(re, '<mark>$1</mark>'); } async function performSearch(query) { const q = query.trim(); if (q.length < 2) { document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = ''; document.getElementById('search-count').textContent = ''; return; } const data = await loadSearchIndex(); const lower = q.toLowerCase(); const matches = []; for (const ch of data) { if (ch.text.toLowerCase().includes(lower)) { matches.push(ch); if (matches.length >= 20) break; } } const count = matches.length; document.getElementById('search-count').textContent = count > 0 ? 'พบ ' + count + ' chapters' + (count >= 20 ? + '</span></div>' + '<div class="sri-snippet">' + snippet + '</div></div>'; } document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = html; } function debouncedSearch(value) { cat > /tmp/patch_base.py << 'EOF' import re, shutil BASE_TAG = ' <base href="/monolith-workspace/">\n' files = [ '/home/sandbox/monolith_site/index.html', '/home/sandbox/monolith_site/analytics.html', '/home/sandbox/monolith_site/changelog.html', ] for path in files: content = open(path).read() # Skip if already patched if '<base href=' in content: print(f'Already patched: {path}') continue # Insert after <head> tag new_content = content.replace('<head>\n', f'<head>\n{BASE_TAG}', 1) if new_content == content: # Try without newline new_content = content.replace('<head>', f'<head>\n{BASE_TAG.rstrip()}', 1) # Save to /tmp/ (modified version for GitHub) fname = path.split('/')[-1] out = f'/tmp/gh_{fname}' with open(out, 'w') as f: f.write(new_content) print(f'Created: {out}') # Verify lines = open(out).readlines() for i, l in enumerate(lines[:6], 1): print(f' {i}: {l}', end='') EOF python3 /tmp/patch_base.py
Push 3 modified HTML files ขึ้น GitHub (Contents API)
Command
cat > /tmp/push_html.py << 'EOF' import requests, base64, json TOKEN = "[CREDENTIAL REDACTED]" REPO = "indetailsgroup-hue/monolith-workspace" BASE = "https://api.github.com" HEAD = { "Authorization": f"token {TOKEN}", "Accept": "application/vnd.github.v3+json", "Content-Type": "application/json" } def b64(path): with open(path, "rb") as f: return base64.b64encode(f.read()).decode() def push_file(local_path, repo_path, message): url = f"{BASE}/repos/{REPO}/contents/{repo_path}" r = requests.get(url, headers=HEAD) payload = {"message": message, "content": b64(local_path), "branch": "main"} if r.status_code == 200: payload["sha"] = r.json()["sha"] action = "UPDATE" else: action = "CREATE" r2 = requests.put(url, headers=HEAD, data=json.dumps(payload)) if r2.status_code in (200, 201): sha = r2.json()['commit']['sha'][:8] print(f" ✅ {action}: {repo_path} → {sha}") else: print(f" ❌ {repo_path} → {r2.status_code}: {r2.text[:200]}") files = [ ("/tmp/gh_index.html", "docs/manufacturing-os-site/index.html", "docs: add <base href> for GitHub Pages subdirectory URL"), ("/tmp/gh_analytics.html", "docs/manufacturing-os-site/analytics.html", "docs: add <base href> to analytics.html for GitHub Pages"), ("/tmp/gh_changelog.html", "docs/manufacturing-os-site/changelog.html", "docs: add <base href> to changelog.html for GitHub Pages"), ] for local, repo_path, msg in files: push_file(local, repo_path, msg) print("\nDone ✅") EOF python3 /tmp/push_html.py

PAT="[CREDENTIAL REDACTED]" REPO="indetailsgroup-hue/monolith-workspace" FILE_PATH="docs/manufacturing-os-site/README.md" # Get current SHA SHA=$(curl -s -H "Authorization: token $PAT" \ "https://api.github.com/repos/$REPO/contents/$FILE_PATH" | \ python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d.get('sha',''))") echo "Current SHA: $SHA" # Base64 encode local file CONTENT=$(base64 -w 0 /home/sandbox/monolith_site/README.md) # Push updated file RESPONSE=$(curl -s -X PUT \ -H "Authorization: token $PAT" \ -H "Content-Type: application/json" \ "https://api.github.com/repos/$REPO/contents/$FILE_PATH" \ -d "{ \"message\": \"docs: add GitHub Actions badges to README.md\", \"content\": \"$CONTENT\", \"sha\": \"$SHA\" }") echo "$RESPONSE" | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print('Commit:', d.get('commit',{}).get('sha','ERROR')[:12], '|', d.get('commit',{}).get('message','')[:60])" 2>/dev/null || echo "$RESPONSE" | head -5

mkdir -p /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows cat > /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/sync-chapters.yml << 'YAML' # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # sync-chapters.yml # Auto-sync chapters.json + search_index.json into docs/manufacturing-os-site/ # whenever a new GitHub Release is published on the monolith-workspace repo. # # Triggers: # • release (published) — automatic sync on every new release # • workflow_dispatch — manual trigger with optional version input # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── name: Sync Chapters on Release on: release: types: [published] workflow_dispatch: inputs: version: description: 'Release version to tag in chapters.json (e.g. v2.1.0)' required: false default: 'manual' dry_run: description: 'Dry run — build but do not commit' required: false default: 'false' type: choice options: ['true', 'false'] permissions: contents: write jobs: sync-chapters: name: Rebuild & Sync Chapter Data runs-on: ubuntu-latest steps: # ── 1. Checkout ───────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Checkout repository uses: actions/checkout@v4 with: fetch-depth: 1 # ── 2. Setup Python ───────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Set up Python 3.11 uses: actions/setup-python@v5 with: python-version: '3.11' # ── 3. Install dependencies ───────────────────────────────────── - name: Install dependencies run: | python -m pip install --upgrade pip pip install beautifulsoup4 lxml # ── 4. Determine release version ──────────────────────────────── - name: Resolve release version id: version run: | if [ "${{ github.event_name }}" = "release" ]; then echo "tag=${{ github.event.release.tag_name }}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT" else echo "tag=${{ github.event.inputs.version }}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT" fi # ── 5. Build chapters.json ─────────────────────────────────────── # Adapt this step to your actual source layout. # Current implementation rebuilds from the existing docs/ markdown/HTML # sources in the repository. Replace the python script path or add your # own content-generation logic here. - name: Build chapters.json id: build-chapters run: | python - << 'PYEOF' import json, os, pathlib, re, hashlib SITE_DIR = pathlib.Path("docs/manufacturing-os-site") OUT_FILE = SITE_DIR / "chapters.json" # ── Locate source files ──────────────────────────────────────────── # Priority 1: markdown files under docs/chapters/ # Priority 2: existing chapters.json (re-stamp only) chapters_src = pathlib.Path("docs/chapters") md_files = sorted(chapters_src.glob("*.md")) if chapters_src.exists() else [] if md_files: print(f"Found {len(md_files)} markdown source files — rebuilding chapters.json") chapters = [] for f in md_files: m = re.match(r'chapter[-_]?(\d+)', f.stem, re.I) num = int(m.group(1)) if m else 0 raw = f.read_text(encoding='utf-8') # Convert minimal markdown → HTML (headings, bold, code) html = re.sub(r'^# (.+)$', r'<h1>\1</h1>', raw, flags=re.M) html = re.sub(r'^## (.+)$', r'<h2>\1</h2>', html, flags=re.M) html = re.sub(r'^### (.+)$',r'<h3>\1</h3>', html, flags=re.M) html = re.sub(r'\*\*(.+?)\*\*', r'<strong>\1</strong>', html) html = re.sub(r'`([^`]+)`', r'<code>\1</code>', html) title_m = re.search(r'<h1>(.+?)</h1>', html) title = title_m.group(1) if title_m else f.stem chapters.append({"num": num, "title": title, "html": html}) chapters.sort(key=lambda c: c["num"]) else: # Re-stamp version in existing chapters.json print("No markdown sources found — re-stamping existing chapters.json") if not OUT_FILE.exists(): print("ERROR: chapters.json not found and no source files present.") raise SystemExit(1) chapters = json.loads(OUT_FILE.read_text(encoding='utf-8')) # ── Write output ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── payload = json.dumps(chapters, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) OUT_FILE.write_text(payload, encoding='utf-8') checksum = hashlib.sha256(payload.encode()).hexdigest()[:12] print(f"chapters.json written — {len(chapters)} chapters · sha256:{checksum}") with open(os.environ['GITHUB_OUTPUT'], 'a') as fh: fh.write(f"chapter_count={len(chapters)}\n") fh.write(f"checksum={checksum}\n") PYEOF # ── 6. Rebuild search_index.json ──────────────────────────────── - name: Build search_index.json run: | python - << 'PYEOF' import json, pathlib, re SITE_DIR = pathlib.Path("docs/manufacturing-os-site") SRC_FILE = SITE_DIR / "chapters.json" OUT_FILE = SITE_DIR / "search_index.json" chapters = json.loads(SRC_FILE.read_text(encoding='utf-8')) index = [] for ch in chapters: plain = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', ch.get('html', '')) plain = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', plain).strip() index.append({ "num": ch.get("num", 0), "title": ch.get("title", ""), "text": plain }) OUT_FILE.write_text( json.dumps(index, ensure_ascii=False), encoding='utf-8' ) print(f"search_index.json written — {len(index)} entries") PYEOF # ── 7. Validate output ─────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Validate JSON outputs run: | python - << 'PYEOF' import json, pathlib, sys SITE = pathlib.Path("docs/manufacturing-os-site") ok = True for fname in ("chapters.json", "search_index.json"): f = SITE / fname if not f.exists(): print(f"FAIL: {fname} missing"); ok = False; continue try: data = json.loads(f.read_text()) print(f"OK: {fname} — {len(data)} entries — {f.stat().st_size:,} bytes") except Exception as e: print(f"FAIL: {fname} invalid JSON — {e}"); ok = False sys.exit(0 if ok else 1) PYEOF # ── 8. Commit & push (skipped on dry_run) ─────────────────────── - name: Commit updated chapter data if: ${{ github.event.inputs.dry_run != 'true' }} run: | git config user.name "github-actions[bot]" git config user.email "github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com" git add docs/manufacturing-os-site/chapters.json \ docs/manufacturing-os-site/search_index.json # Only commit if there are actual changes if git diff --cached --quiet; then echo "No changes to chapters.json or search_index.json — skipping commit." exit 0 fi VERSION="${{ steps.version.outputs.tag }}" COUNT="${{ steps.build-chapters.outputs.chapter_count }}" SUM="${{ steps.build-chapters.outputs.checksum }}" git commit -m "chore(docs): sync chapters.json on release ${VERSION} - Chapters: ${COUNT} - SHA256: ${SUM} - Triggered by: ${{ github.event_name }} - Run: ${{ github.run_id }}" git push origin main echo "✅ chapters.json + search_index.json pushed to main" # ── 9. Summary ────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Write job summary if: always() run: | echo "## Sync Chapters — Job Summary" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Field | Value |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "|---|---|" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Trigger | ${{ github.event_name }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Version | ${{ steps.version.outputs.tag }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Chapters | ${{ steps.build-chapters.outputs.chapter_count }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Checksum | ${{ steps.build-chapters.outputs.checksum }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Dry Run | ${{ github.event.inputs.dry_run || 'false' }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Repo | ${{ github.repository }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Run ID | ${{ github.run_id }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" YAML echo "--- sync-chapters.yml created ---" wc -l /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/sync-chapters.yml

Command
cat > /tmp/new_script.js << 'JSEOF' <script> // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Monolith Status Page — localStorage-backed uptime history // Key: monolith-status-checks // Format: [{ts:number, ep:{id:{ok:boolean,ms:number|null}}}] // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const LAST_DEPLOY = new Date('2026-09-21T04:21:14.093Z'); const LS_KEY = 'monolith-status-checks'; const MAX_ENTRIES = 8640; // 90 days × 96 checks/day (every 15 min) const ENDPOINTS = [ { id: 'scispace', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'analytics', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co/analytics.html', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'changelog', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co/changelog.html', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'ghpages', url: 'https://indetailsgroup-hue.github.io/monolith-workspace/', mode: 'no-cors' }, ]; // ── localStorage helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────── function loadHistory() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY) || '[]'); } catch { return []; } } function appendCheck(ts, epResult) { const h = loadHistory(); h.push({ ts, ep: epResult }); if (h.length > MAX_ENTRIES) h.splice(0, h.length - MAX_ENTRIES); try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, JSON.stringify(h)); } catch {} } // ── Uptime engine ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function computeUptimeData() { const h = loadHistory(); const DAYS = 90; const byDay = {}; // 'YYYY-MM-DD' → [{ok:bool}] for (const check of h) { const key = new Date(check.ts).toISOString().slice(0, 10); const vals = Object.values(check.ep || {}); const allOk = vals.length > 0 && vals.every(v => v.ok); if (!byDay[key]) byDay[key] = []; byDay[key].push(allOk); } const days = []; let operationalDays = 0, dataDays = 0; for (let i = 0; i < DAYS; i++) { const date = new Date(Date.now() - (DAYS - 1 - i) * 86400000); const key = date.toISOString().slice(0, 10); const checks = byDay[key]; if (!checks || checks.length === 0) { days.push({ key, status: 'nodata', pct: null, count: 0 }); } else { dataDays++; const ok = checks.filter(Boolean).length; const pct = (ok / checks.length) * 100; const status = pct >= 99 ? 'ok' : pct >= 50 ? 'degraded' : 'outage'; if (status === 'ok') operationalDays++; days.push({ key, status, pct, count: checks.length }); } } const uptimePct = dataDays > 0 ? ((operationalDays / dataDays) * 100).toFixed(2) : null; return { days, uptimePct, totalChecks: h.length, dataDays, firstTs: h.length ? h[0].ts : null, lastTs: h.length ? h[h.length - 1].ts : null }; } // ── Render uptime blocks ─────────────────────────────────────────────── function renderUptimeBlocks(data) { const container = document.getElementById('uptime-blocks'); if (!container) return; container.innerHTML = ''; const COLOR = { ok: 'var(--green)', degraded:'var(--yellow)', outage: 'var(--red)', nodata: 'var(--border)', }; const frag = document.createDocumentFragment(); for (const day of data.days) { const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'ub'; d.style.background = COLOR[day.status]; if (day.status === 'nodata') d.style.opacity = '1'; const label = day.status === 'nodata' ? 'No data' : `${day.pct.toFixed(1)}% uptime (${day.count} checks)`; d.title = `${day.key}: ${label}`; frag.appendChild(d); } container.appendChild(frag); // Progress bar const fillEl = document.getElementById('uptime-fill'); const pctLabel = document.getElementById('uptime-pct-label'); if (data.uptimePct !== null) { if (fillEl) fillEl.style.width = data.uptimePct + '%'; if (pctLabel) pctLabel.textContent = data.uptimePct + '% uptime'; document.getElementById('overall-pct').textContent = data.uptimePct + '%'; } else { if (fillEl) fillEl.style.width = '0%'; if (pctLabel) pctLabel.textContent = 'No history yet'; } } // ── Render history stats row ─────────────────────────────────────────── function renderHistoryStats(data) { const $ = id => document.getElementById(id); const fmtTs = ts => ts ? new Date(ts).toLocaleString('en-GB', { day:'2-digit', month:'short', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit' }) : '—'; $('stat-total').textContent = data.totalChecks.toLocaleString(); $('stat-first').textContent = fmtTs(data.firstTs); $('stat-last' ).textContent = fmtTs(data.lastTs); $('stat-days' ).textContent = data.dataDays; } // ── Render recent checks table ───────────────────────────────────────── function renderRecentChecks() { const h = loadHistory(); const last = h.slice(-10).reverse(); const tbody = document.getElementById('recent-checks-body'); if (!tbody) return; if (last.length === 0) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" style="padding:12px 14px;color:var(--muted);text-align:center;">No checks recorded yet</td></tr>'; return; } const IDS = ['scispace', 'analytics', 'changelog', 'ghpages']; tbody.innerHTML = last.map((c, idx) => { const ts = new Date(c.ts).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit', second:'2-digit' }); const date = new Date(c.ts).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day:'2-digit', month:'short' }); const cells = IDS.map(id => { const r = (c.ep || {})[id]; if (!r) return `<td style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);">—</td>`; const color = r.ok ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)'; const label = r.ok ? (r.ms ? r.ms + 'ms' : 'OK') : 'ERR'; return `<td style="text-align:center;color:${color};font-family:monospace;">${label}</td>`; }).join(''); const rowBg = idx % 2 === 0 ? '' : 'background:#191c28;'; return `<tr style="${rowBg}"> <td style="padding:7px 14px;color:var(--muted);">${date} ${ts}</td> ${cells} </tr>`; }).join(''); } // ── Relative time ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function relativeTime(date) { const s = Math.floor((Date.now() - date) / 1000); if (s < 60) return `${s}s ago`; if (s < 3600) return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}m ago`; if (s < 86400) return `${Math.floor(s / 3600)}h ago`; return `${Math.floor(s / 86400)}d ago`; } function updateDeployAge() { const el = document.getElementById('last-deploy-rel'); if (el) el.textContent = 'Deployed ' + relativeTime(LAST_DEPLOY); } updateDeployAge(); setInterval(updateDeployAge, 30000); // ── Health check ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function checkEndpoint(ep) { const t0 = performance.now(); try { await fetch(ep.url, { method: 'HEAD', mode: ep.mode, cache: 'no-cache' }); return { ok: true, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; } catch { if (ep.mode === 'no-cors') return { ok: true, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; return { ok: false, ms: null }; } } function setBadge(id, ok, ms) { const badge = document.getElementById('badge-' + id); const ping = document.getElementById('ping-' + id); if (!badge || !ping) return; if (ok) { badge.className = 'badge operational'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Operational'; ping.querySelector('.val').textContent = ms ? ms + 'ms' : 'OK'; } else { badge.className = 'badge outage'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Unreachable'; ping.querySelector('.val').textContent = '—'; } } // ── Full refresh cycle ──────────────────────────────────────────────── async function runChecks() { const now = new Date(); document.getElementById('last-checked').textContent = now.toTimeString().slice(0, 8); // Run all endpoint checks const results = await Promise.all(ENDPOINTS.map(ep => checkEndpoint(ep))); // Build ep map and update badges const epMap = {}; let allOk = true; results.forEach((r, i) => { const ep = ENDPOINTS[i]; epMap[ep.id] = r; setBadge(ep.id, r.ok, r.ms); if (!r.ok) allOk = false; }); // Persist to localStorage appendCheck(now.getTime(), epMap); // Refresh UI const data = computeUptimeData(); renderUptimeBlocks(data); renderHistoryStats(data); renderRecentChecks(); // Update overall banner const banner = document.getElementById('overall-banner'); const label = document.getElementById('overall-label'); const ring = document.getElementById('pulse-ring'); const pct = document.getElementById('overall-pct'); if (allOk) { banner.className = 'overall-banner'; label.textContent = 'All Systems Operational'; label.style.color = 'var(--green)'; ring.style.background = 'var(--green)'; pct.style.color = 'var(--green)'; } else { banner.className = 'overall-banner degraded'; label.textContent = 'Partial Outage Detected'; label.style.color = 'var(--yellow)'; ring.style.background = 'var(--yellow)'; pct.style.color = 'var(--yellow)'; } } // ── Auto-refresh countdown ───────────────────────────────────────────── let countdown = 60; setInterval(() => { countdown--; const el = document.getElementById('countdown'); if (el) el.textContent = countdown; if (countdown <= 0) { countdown = 60; runChecks(); } }, 1000); // ── Clear history button ─────────────────────────────────────────────── document.getElementById('btn-clear-history')?.addEventListener('click', () => { if (confirm('Clear all stored uptime history? cat > /tmp/status_script_v3.js << 'JSEOF' <script> // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Monolith Status Page v3 — localStorage uptime + JSON data-layer checks // Keys: // monolith-status-checks → [{ts, ep:{id:{ok,ms}}, json:{id:{ok,count,kb,ms,err}}}] // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const LAST_DEPLOY = new Date('2026-09-21T04:27:56.183Z'); const LS_KEY = 'monolith-status-checks'; const MAX_ENTRIES = 8640; const EXPECTED_CHAPTERS = 55; // ── Endpoint definitions ────────────────────────────────────────────── const ENDPOINTS = [ { id: 'scispace', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'analytics', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co/analytics.html', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'changelog', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co/changelog.html', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'ghpages', url: 'https://indetailsgroup-hue.github.io/monolith-workspace/', mode: 'no-cors' }, ]; const JSON_ENDPOINTS = [ { id: 'chapters', url: 'chapters.json', label: 'chapters', expectedCount: EXPECTED_CHAPTERS }, { id: 'search', url: 'search_index.json', label: 'entries', expectedCount: EXPECTED_CHAPTERS }, ]; // ── localStorage helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────── function loadHistory() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY) || '[]'); } catch { return []; } } function appendCheck(ts, epMap, jsonMap) { const h = loadHistory(); h.push({ ts, ep: epMap, json: jsonMap }); if (h.length > MAX_ENTRIES) h.splice(0, h.length - MAX_ENTRIES); try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, JSON.stringify(h)); } catch {} } // ── Uptime engine (uses only `ep` for HTTP uptime calc) ─────────────── function computeUptimeData() { const h = loadHistory(), DAYS = 90; const byDay = {}; for (const c of h) { const k = new Date(c.ts).toISOString().slice(0, 10); const vals = Object.values(c.ep || {}); const allOk = vals.length > 0 && vals.every(v => v.ok); (byDay[k] = byDay[k] || []).push(allOk); } const days = []; let opDays = 0, dataDays = 0; for (let i = 0; i < DAYS; i++) { const date = new Date(Date.now() - (DAYS - 1 - i) * 86400000); const k = date.toISOString().slice(0, 10); const chk = byDay[k]; if (!chk) { days.push({ k, status: 'nodata', pct: null, n: 0 }); continue; } dataDays++; const ok = chk.filter(Boolean).length; const pct = (ok / chk.length) * 100; const status = pct >= 99 ? 'ok' : pct >= 50 ? 'degraded' : 'outage'; if (status === 'ok') opDays++; days.push({ k, status, pct, n: chk.length }); } const uptimePct = dataDays > 0 ? ((opDays / dataDays) * 100).toFixed(2) : null; return { days, uptimePct, dataDays, totalChecks: h.length, firstTs: h.length ? h[0].ts : null, lastTs: h.length ? h[h.length-1].ts : null, }; } // ── Render uptime blocks ────────────────────────────────────────────── function renderUptimeBlocks(data) { const C = { ok:'var(--green)', degraded:'var(--yellow)', outage:'var(--red)', nodata:'var(--border)' }; const container = document.getElementById('uptime-blocks'); if (!container) return; container.innerHTML = ''; const frag = document.createDocumentFragment(); for (const d of data.days) { const el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'ub'; el.style.background = C[d.status]; if (d.status === 'nodata') el.style.opacity = '1'; el.title = d.status === 'nodata' ? `${d.k}: No data` : `${d.k}: ${d.pct.toFixed(1)}% uptime (${d.n} checks)`; frag.appendChild(el); } container.appendChild(frag); const fill = document.getElementById('uptime-fill'); const label = document.getElementById('uptime-pct-label'); const pctEl = document.getElementById('overall-pct'); if (data.uptimePct !== null) { if (fill) fill.style.width = data.uptimePct + '%'; if (label) label.textContent = data.uptimePct + '% uptime'; if (pctEl) pctEl.textContent = data.uptimePct + '%'; } else { if (fill) fill.style.width = '0%'; if (label) label.textContent = 'No history yet'; } } // ── Render history stats ────────────────────────────────────────────── function renderHistoryStats(data) { const $ = id => document.getElementById(id); const fmt = ts => ts ? new Date(ts).toLocaleString('en-GB', { day:'2-digit', month:'short', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit' }) : '—'; $('stat-total').textContent = data.totalChecks.toLocaleString(); $('stat-first').textContent = fmt(data.firstTs); $('stat-last' ).textContent = fmt(data.lastTs); $('stat-days' ).textContent = data.dataDays; } // ── Render recent checks table ──────────────────────────────────────── const EP_IDS = ['scispace', 'analytics', 'changelog', 'ghpages']; const JSON_IDS = ['chapters', 'search']; function renderRecentChecks() { const h = loadHistory().slice(-10).reverse(); const tbody = document.getElementById('recent-checks-body'); if (!tbody) return; if (!h.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" style="padding:12px 14px;color:var(--muted);text-align:center;">No checks recorded yet</td></tr>'; return; } tbody.innerHTML = h.map((c, idx) => { const ts = new Date(c.ts).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit', second:'2-digit' }); const date = new Date(c.ts).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day:'2-digit', month:'short' }); const epCells = EP_IDS.map(id => { const r = (c.ep || {})[id]; if (!r) return `<td style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);">—</td>`; const color = r.ok ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)'; return `<td style="text-align:center;color:${color};font-family:monospace;">${r.ok ? (r.ms || 'OK') + (r.ms ? 'ms' : '') : 'ERR'}</td>`; }).join(''); const jsonCells = JSON_IDS.map(id => { const r = (c.json || {})[id]; if (!r) return `<td style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);">—</td>`; if (r.err) return `<td style="text-align:center;color:var(--red);font-family:monospace;font-size:10px;">ERR</td>`; const color = r.ok ? '#2dd4bf' : 'var(--yellow)'; return `<td style="text-align:center;color:${color};font-family:monospace;">${r.count}ch</td>`; }).join(''); const bg = idx % 2 === 0 ? '' : 'background:#191c28;'; return `<tr style="${bg}"> <td style="padding:7px 14px;color:var(--muted);">${date} ${ts}</td> ${epCells}${jsonCells} </tr>`; }).join(''); } // ── Relative time ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── function relativeTime(d) { const s = Math.floor((Date.now() - d) / 1000); if (s < 60) return s + 's ago'; if (s < 3600) return Math.floor(s/60) + 'm ago'; if (s < 86400) return Math.floor(s/3600) + 'h ago'; return Math.floor(s/86400) + 'd ago'; } function updateDeployAge() { const el = document.getElementById('last-deploy-rel'); if (el) el.textContent = 'Deployed ' + relativeTime(LAST_DEPLOY); } updateDeployAge(); setInterval(updateDeployAge, 30000); // ── HTTP endpoint check ─────────────────────────────────────────────── async function checkEndpoint(ep) { const t0 = performance.now(); try { await fetch(ep.url, { method: 'HEAD', mode: ep.mode, cache: 'no-cache' }); return { ok: true, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; } catch { if (ep.mode === 'no-cors') return { ok: true, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; return { ok: false, ms: null }; } } // ── JSON endpoint check ─────────────────────────────────────────────── async function checkJsonEndpoint(ep) { const t0 = performance.now(); try { const resp = await fetch(ep.url + '?_t=' + Date.now(), { cache: 'no-cache' }); if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status); const sizeBytes = parseInt(resp.headers.get('content-length') || '0', 10); const text = await resp.text(); const data = JSON.parse(text); const ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0); const kb = sizeBytes > 0 ? (sizeBytes / 1024).toFixed(0) : (text.length / 1024).toFixed(0); const count = Array.isArray(data) ? data.length : Object.keys(data).length; const ok = count >= ep.expectedCount; const warn = !ok && count > 0; return { ok, warn, ms, count, kb, err: null }; } catch (e) { return { ok: false, warn: false, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0), count: 0, kb: 0, err: e.message }; } } // ── HTTP badge ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function setBadge(id, ok, ms) { const badge = document.getElementById('badge-' + id); const val = document.querySelector('#ping-' + id + ' .val') || document.getElementById('ping-' + id)?.querySelector('.val'); if (!badge) return; if (ok) { badge.className = 'badge operational'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Operational'; if (val) val.textContent = ms ? ms + 'ms' : 'OK'; } else { badge.className = 'badge outage'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Unreachable'; if (val) val.textContent = '—'; } } // ── JSON badge ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function setBadgeJson(id, result, labelWord) { const badge = document.getElementById('badge-' + id); const valEl = document.getElementById('ping-' + id); const labelEl = document.getElementById('ping-' + id + '-label'); const sizeEl = document.getElementById('ping-' + id + '-size'); if (!badge) return; if (result.err) { badge.className = 'badge outage'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Parse Error'; if (valEl) valEl.querySelector('.val').textContent = 'ERR'; if (labelEl) labelEl.textContent = result.err.slice(0, 30); if (sizeEl) sizeEl.textContent = ''; } else if (result.warn) { badge.className = 'badge warn'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Incomplete'; if (valEl) valEl.querySelector('.val').textContent = result.count; if (labelEl) labelEl.textContent = `${labelWord} (exp. ${EXPECTED_CHAPTERS})`; if (sizeEl) sizeEl.textContent = result.kb + ' KB · ' + result.ms + 'ms'; } else { badge.className = 'badge valid-json'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Valid JSON ✓'; if (valEl) valEl.querySelector('.val').textContent = result.count; if (labelEl) labelEl.textContent = labelWord; if (sizeEl) sizeEl.textContent = result.kb + ' KB · ' + result.ms + 'ms'; } } // ── Full check cycle ────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function runChecks() { const now = new Date(); const lastEl = document.getElementById('last-checked'); if (lastEl) lastEl.textContent = now.toTimeString().slice(0, 8); // Run HTTP + JSON checks concurrently const [httpResults, jsonResults] = await Promise.all([ Promise.all(ENDPOINTS.map(ep => checkEndpoint(ep))), Promise.all(JSON_ENDPOINTS.map(ep => checkJsonEndpoint(ep))), ]); // Process HTTP results const epMap = {}; let allHttpOk = true; httpResults.forEach((r, i) => { const ep = ENDPOINTS[i]; epMap[ep.id] = r; setBadge(ep.id, r.ok, r.ms); if (!r.ok) allHttpOk = false; }); // Process JSON results const jsonMap = {}; let allJsonOk = true; jsonResults.forEach((r, i) => { const ep = JSON_ENDPOINTS[i]; jsonMap[ep.id] = r; setBadgeJson(ep.id, r, ep.label); if (!r.ok) allJsonOk = false; }); // Persist appendCheck(now.getTime(), epMap, jsonMap); // Refresh dashboard const data = computeUptimeData(); renderUptimeBlocks(data); renderHistoryStats(data); renderRecentChecks(); // Overall banner const banner = document.getElementById('overall-banner'); const label = document.getElementById('overall-label'); const ring = document.getElementById('pulse-ring'); const pctEl = document.getElementById('overall-pct'); const allOk = allHttpOk && allJsonOk; if (allOk) { banner.className = 'overall-banner'; label.textContent = 'All Systems Operational'; label.style.color = 'var(--green)'; ring.style.background = 'var(--green)'; if (pctEl) pctEl.style.color = 'var(--green)'; } else { banner.className = 'overall-banner degraded'; label.textContent = allHttpOk ? 'Data Layer Warning' : 'Partial Outage Detected'; label.style.color = 'var(--yellow)'; ring.style.background = 'var(--yellow)'; if (pctEl) pctEl.style.color = 'var(--yellow)'; } } // ── Update recent-checks table header (add chapters/search cols) ────── (function patchTableHeader() { const thead = document.querySelector('#recent-checks-body') ?.closest('table')?.querySelector('thead tr'); if (!thead) return; const extra = ['chapters.json', 'search.json']; extra.forEach(label => { const th = document.createElement('th'); th.style.cssText = 'padding:8px 8px;text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-weight:500;'; th.textContent = label; thead.appendChild(th); }); })(); // ── Auto-refresh countdown ──────────────────────────────────────────── let countdown = 60; setInterval(() => { countdown--; const el = document.getElementById('countdown'); if (el) el.textContent = countdown; if (countdown <= 0) { countdown = 60; runChecks(); } }, 1000); // ── Clear history ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── document.getElementById('btn-clear-history')?.addEventListener('click', () => { if (!confirm('Clear all stored uptime history?')) return; localStorage.removeItem(LS_KEY); const data = computeUptimeData(); renderUptimeBlocks(data); renderHistoryStats(data); renderRecentChecks(); }); // ── Boot ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── (function boot() { const data = computeUptimeData(); renderUptimeBlocks(data); renderHistoryStats(data); renderRecentChecks(); })(); runChecks(); </script> JSEOF echo "Script v3: $(wc -l /tmp/status_script_v3.js) lines"

mkdir -p /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows cat > /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/notify-slack-deploy.yml << 'YAML' # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # notify-slack-deploy.yml # Sends a Slack notification whenever deploy-docs-pages.yml completes. # # Required secret: # SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL — Slack Incoming Webhook URL # (Settings → Secrets and variables → Actions → New repository secret) # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── name: Notify Slack on Deploy on: workflow_run: workflows: - "Deploy Manufacturing OS Docs" # must match `name:` in deploy-docs-pages.yml types: - completed jobs: slack-notify: name: Send Slack Notification runs-on: ubuntu-latest # Run for both success and failure if: ${{ github.event.workflow_run.conclusion == 'success' || github.event.workflow_run.conclusion == 'failure' }} steps: - name: Prepare payload id: payload run: | CONCLUSION="${{ github.event.workflow_run.conclusion }}" RUN_ID="${{ github.event.workflow_run.id }}" RUN_URL="https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${RUN_ID}" COMMIT_SHA="${{ github.event.workflow_run.head_sha }}" COMMIT_SHORT="${COMMIT_SHA:0:7}" COMMIT_URL="https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/commit/${COMMIT_SHA}" SITE_URL="https://indetailsgroup-hue.github.io/monolith-workspace/" BRANCH="${{ github.event.workflow_run.head_branch }}" ACTOR="${{ github.event.workflow_run.actor.login }}" TRIGGERED_AT="${{ github.event.workflow_run.run_started_at }}" if [ "$CONCLUSION" = "success" ]; then EMOJI=":white_check_mark:" STATUS_TEXT="Deploy *succeeded*" COLOR="good" else EMOJI=":x:" STATUS_TEXT="Deploy *failed*" COLOR="danger" fi # Write JSON payload to file (avoids shell escaping issues) cat > /tmp/slack_payload.json << JSON { "attachments": [ { "color": "${COLOR}", "fallback": "${STATUS_TEXT} — Monolith Manufacturing OS Docs (${BRANCH})", "blocks": [ { "type": "header", "text": { "type": "plain_text", "text": "${EMOJI} Monolith Manufacturing OS — Docs Deploy" } }, { "type": "section", "fields": [ { "type": "mrkdwn", "text": "*Status:*\n${STATUS_TEXT}" }, { "type": "mrkdwn", "text": "*Branch:*\n\`${BRANCH}\`" }, { "type": "mrkdwn", "text": "*Commit:*\n<${COMMIT_URL}|\`${COMMIT_SHORT}\`>" }, { "type": "mrkdwn", "text": "*Triggered by:*\n${ACTOR}" } ] }, { "type": "actions", "elements": [ { "type": "button", "text": { "type": "plain_text", "text": "View Run" }, "url": "${RUN_URL}" }, { "type": "button", "text": { "type": "plain_text", "text": "Open Site" }, "url": "${SITE_URL}" } ] } ] } ] } JSON echo "payload_file=/tmp/slack_payload.json" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT - name: Send to Slack run: | HTTP_STATUS=$(curl -s -o /tmp/slack_response.txt -w "%{http_code}" \ -X POST \ -H "Content-Type: application/json" \ --data @${{ steps.payload.outputs.payload_file }} \ "${{ secrets.SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL }}") echo "Slack response HTTP ${HTTP_STATUS}: $(cat /tmp/slack_response.txt)" if [ "$HTTP_STATUS" != "200" ]; then echo "::error::Slack notification failed with HTTP ${HTTP_STATUS}" exit 1 fi YAML echo "Created: $(wc -l < /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/notify-slack-deploy.yml) lines"

Resolve target URL ──────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Resolve audit URL id: url run: | if [ -n "${{ github.event.inputs.site_url }}" ]; then URL="${{ github.event.inputs.site_url }}" else URL="https://indetailsgroup-hue.github.io/monolith-workspace/" fi echo "url=$URL" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "Auditing: $URL" # ── 2. Wait for GitHub Pages to propagate (only on workflow_run) ───────── - name: Wait for Pages propagation if: github.event_name == 'workflow_run' run: | echo "Waiting 30s for GitHub Pages to propagate…" sleep 30 # Verify the page is actually reachable HTTP=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" "${{ steps.url.outputs.url }}") echo "HTTP status: $HTTP" if [ "$HTTP" != "200" ]; then echo "::warning::Site returned HTTP $HTTP — audit may be stale" fi # ── 3. Install Lighthouse CI ───────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Install Lighthouse CI run: npm install -g @lhci/cli@0.14.x # ── 4. Run Lighthouse audit ────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Run Lighthouse id: lh run: | mkdir -p /tmp/lhci-results lhci collect \ --url="${{ steps.url.outputs.url }}" \ --numberOfRuns=1 \ --settings.output=json \ --settings.outputPath=/tmp/lhci-results/report.json \ --settings.chromeFlags="--no-sandbox --headless" \ || true # Also produce HTML report lhci collect \ --url="${{ steps.url.outputs.url }}" \ --numberOfRuns=1 \ --settings.output=html \ --settings.outputPath=/tmp/lhci-results/report.html \ --settings.chromeFlags="--no-sandbox --headless" \ || true # Parse scores from JSON report JSON_FILE=$(ls /tmp/lhci-results/*.json 2>/dev/null | head -1) if [ -z "$JSON_FILE" ]; then echo "::error::Lighthouse JSON report not found" echo "perf=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "a11y=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "bp=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "seo=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "fcp=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "lcp=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "tbt=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "cls=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "tti=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT exit 0 fi python3 << 'PYEOF' import json, os, sys with open(os.environ.get('JSON_FILE', '')) or open(sys.argv[1]) as f: data = json.load(f) cats = data.get('categories', {}) audits = data.get('audits', {}) def score(cat_key): val = cats.get(cat_key, {}).get('score') return f"{int(val * 100)}" if val is not None else 'N/A' def metric(audit_key, unit='ms'): val = audits.get(audit_key, {}).get('numericValue') if val is None: return 'N/A' if unit == 'ms': return f"{val/1000:.1f}s" if val >= 1000 else f"{int(val)}ms" if unit == 's': return f"{val:.2f}s" return str(round(val, 3)) def emoji(s): if s == 'N/A': return '⚪' n = int(s) if n >= 90: return '🟢' if n >= 50: return '🟡' return '🔴' perf = score('performance') a11y = score('accessibility') bp = score('best-practices') seo = score('seo') fcp = metric('first-contentful-paint') lcp = metric('largest-contentful-paint') tbt = metric('total-blocking-time') cls = metric('cumulative-layout-shift', 'raw') if audits.get('cumulative-layout-shift') else 'N/A' tti = metric('interactive') out = f""" perf={perf} a11y={a11y} bp={bp} seo={seo} fcp={fcp} lcp={lcp} tbt={tbt} cls={cls} tti={tti} perf_emoji={emoji(perf)} a11y_emoji={emoji(a11y)} bp_emoji={emoji(bp)} seo_emoji={emoji(seo)} """.strip() with open(os.environ['GITHUB_OUTPUT'], 'a') as f: f.write(out + '\n') print("Scores parsed:", perf, a11y, bp, seo) PYEOF env: JSON_FILE: ${{ steps.lh.outputs.json_file }} # ── 5. Upload HTML report as artifact ─────────────────────────────────── - name: Upload Lighthouse report uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4 with: name: lighthouse-report path: /tmp/lhci-results/ retention-days: 30 # ── 6. Find associated PR ──────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Find associated PR id: pr uses: actions/github-script@v7 with: script: | // For workflow_run, use the head SHA from the triggering run const sha = context.eventName === 'workflow_run' ? context.payload.workflow_run.head_sha : context.sha; console.log(`Looking for PR with SHA: ${sha}`); const prs = await github.rest.pulls.list({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, state: 'open', per_page: 20, }); const match = prs.data.find(pr => pr.head.sha === sha); if (match) { console.log(`Found PR #${match.number}: ${match.title}`); return { pr_number: match.number, found: true }; } // No open PR — try merged PRs with this SHA const merged = await github.rest.pulls.list({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, state: 'closed', per_page: 10, }); const mergedMatch = merged.data.find(pr => pr.merge_commit_sha === sha || pr.head.sha === sha); if (mergedMatch) { console.log(`Found merged PR #${mergedMatch.number}`); return { pr_number: mergedMatch.number, found: true }; } console.log('No associated PR found — will skip PR comment'); return { pr_number: null, found: false }; # ── 7. Build comment body ──────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Build comment body id: comment run: | RUN_URL="https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${{ github.run_id }}" SITE_URL="${{ steps.url.outputs.url }}" COMMIT_SHA="${{ github.event.workflow_run.head_sha || github.sha }}" COMMIT_SHORT="${COMMIT_SHA:0:7}" cat > /tmp/comment_body.md << COMMENT ## 🔦 Lighthouse Audit — \`${COMMIT_SHORT}\` Audited: [\`${SITE_URL}\`](${SITE_URL}) | Category | Score | | |---|---|---| | Performance | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.perf }} | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.perf_emoji }} | | Accessibility | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.a11y }} | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.a11y_emoji }} | | Best Practices | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.bp }} | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.bp_emoji }} | | SEO | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.seo }} | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.seo_emoji }} | **Core Web Vitals** | Metric | Value | |---|---| | First Contentful Paint (FCP) | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.fcp }} | | Largest Contentful Paint (LCP) | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.lcp }} | | Total Blocking Time (TBT) | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.tbt }} | | Cumulative Layout Shift (CLS) | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.cls }} | | Time to Interactive (TTI) | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.tti }} | > Score legend: 🟢 ≥ 90 · 🟡 50–89 · 🔴 < 50 > > Full HTML report: [Download from Actions artifacts](${RUN_URL}) COMMENT echo "body_file=/tmp/comment_body.md" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT # ── 8. Post comment to PR ──────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Post PR comment if: fromJson(steps.pr.outputs.result).found == true uses: actions/github-script@v7 with: script: | const fs = require('fs'); const body = fs.readFileSync('${{ steps.comment.outputs.body_file }}', 'utf8'); const prNumber = ${{ fromJson(steps.pr.outputs.result).pr_number }}; // Find and update existing Lighthouse comment, or create new one const comments = await github.rest.issues.listComments({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, issue_number: prNumber, }); const existing = comments.data.find(c => c.user.type === 'Bot' && c.body.includes('🔦 Lighthouse Audit') ); if (existing) { await github.rest.issues.updateComment({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, comment_id: existing.id, body, }); console.log(`Updated Lighthouse comment #${existing.id} on PR #${prNumber}`); } else { await github.rest.issues.createComment({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, issue_number: prNumber, body, }); console.log(`Created Lighthouse comment on PR #${prNumber}`); } # ── 9. Print summary (when no PR found) ───────────────────────────────── - name: Print audit summary if: always() run: | echo "## Lighthouse Audit Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY cat /tmp/comment_body.md >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY || echo "No report generated" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY YAML echo "Created: $(wc -l < /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/lighthouse-audit.yml) lines"

Task 3 — สร้าง generate-site-data.yml
Command
cat > /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/generate-site-data.yml << 'YAML' # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # generate-site-data.yml # Auto-generates chapters.json and search_index.json from markdown source files. # # Source convention: # docs/chapters/chapter-{N}.md — one file per chapter, N = 1..55 # Frontmatter (optional): # --- # num: 1 # title: บทสรุปสำหรับผู้บริหาร # phase: 1 # mcp_tools: 3 # status: complete # --- # # Outputs (committed back to repo): # docs/manufacturing-os-site/chapters.json — {num_str: html, ...} # docs/manufacturing-os-site/search_index.json — [{num, text}, ...] # # Triggers: # - push: changes to docs/chapters/**/*.md # - workflow_dispatch: manual run, optionally override source/output paths # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── name: Generate Site Data on: push: branches: [main] paths: - "docs/chapters/**/*.md" - "docs/chapters/*.md" workflow_dispatch: inputs: chapters_path: description: "Path to chapter markdown files (relative to repo root)" required: false default: "docs/chapters" output_path: description: "Output directory for JSON files (relative to repo root)" required: false default: "docs/manufacturing-os-site" dry_run: description: "Dry run — generate but do not commit (true/false)" required: false default: "false" permissions: contents: write env: CHAPTERS_PATH: ${{ github.event.inputs.chapters_path || 'docs/chapters' }} OUTPUT_PATH: ${{ github.event.inputs.output_path || 'docs/manufacturing-os-site' }} DRY_RUN: ${{ github.event.inputs.dry_run || 'false' }} jobs: generate: name: Generate chapters.json & search_index.json runs-on: ubuntu-latest steps: # ── 1. Checkout ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Checkout repository uses: actions/checkout@v4 with: token: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }} fetch-depth: 0 # ── 2. Set up Python ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Set up Python uses: actions/setup-python@v5 with: python-version: "3.12" - name: Install Python dependencies run: pip install markdown PyYAML # ── 3. Check source directory ──────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Check source directory id: check run: | CHAPTERS_DIR="${CHAPTERS_PATH}" if [ ! -d "$CHAPTERS_DIR" ]; then echo "::warning::Source directory '${CHAPTERS_DIR}' does not exist — creating with example file" mkdir -p "$CHAPTERS_DIR" # Create example chapter file so the directory is not empty cat > "${CHAPTERS_DIR}/chapter-01.md" << 'MD' --- num: 1 title: บทสรุปสำหรับผู้บริหาร phase: 1 mcp_tools: 3 status: complete --- # 1. บทสรุปสำหรับผู้บริหาร > เพิ่มเนื้อหา chapter ที่นี่ MD echo "created_example=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT fi COUNT=$(find "$CHAPTERS_DIR" -name "*.md" | wc -l) echo "Found ${COUNT} markdown file(s) in ${CHAPTERS_DIR}" echo "count=${COUNT}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT # ── 4. Generate JSON files ──────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Generate chapters.json and search_index.json id: generate run: | python3 - << 'PYEOF' import os, re, json, html as html_mod import markdown import yaml CHAPTERS_DIR = os.environ['CHAPTERS_PATH'] OUTPUT_DIR = os.environ['OUTPUT_PATH'] CHAPTERS_JSON = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, 'chapters.json') SEARCH_JSON = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, 'search_index.json') # ── Parse frontmatter ──────────────────────────────────────────────── FRONT_RE = re.compile(r'^---\s*\n(.*?)\n---\s*\n', re.DOTALL) def parse_file(path): with open(path, encoding='utf-8') as f: raw = f.read() meta = {} body = raw m = FRONT_RE.match(raw) if m: try: meta = yaml.safe_load(m.group(1)) or {} except Exception: meta = {} body = raw[m.end():] return meta, body # ── Infer chapter number from filename or frontmatter ──────────────── def get_num(meta, filename): if 'num' in meta: return int(meta['num']) # chapter-01.md, chapter-1.md, 01-intro.md, 1.md m = re.search(r'(\d+)', os.path.basename(filename)) return int(m.group(1)) if m else None # ── Convert markdown to HTML ───────────────────────────────────────── md = markdown.Markdown( extensions=['extra', 'toc', 'codehilite', 'nl2br'], extension_configs={ 'toc': {'permalink': True}, 'codehilite': {'guess_lang': False}, } ) # ── Collect and sort chapters ──────────────────────────────────────── files = sorted( [f for f in os.listdir(CHAPTERS_DIR) if f.endswith('.md')], key=lambda f: (get_num({}, f) or 9999, f) ) chapters_dict = {} search_entries = [] skipped = [] for fname in files: path = os.path.join(CHAPTERS_DIR, fname) meta, body = parse_file(path) num = get_num(meta, fname) if num is None: skipped.append(fname) continue md.reset() html_content = md.convert(body) # Add heading id anchor from chapter number if not present if not html_content.startswith('<h1'): title = meta.get('title', f'Chapter {num}') html_content = f'<h1 id="{num}">{num}. {title}</h1>\n' + html_content chapters_dict[str(num)] = html_content # Build plain-text search entry plain = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', html_content) plain = html_mod.unescape(plain) plain = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', plain).strip() search_entries.append({'num': num, 'text': plain[:6000]}) search_entries.sort(key=lambda x: x['num']) # ── Write output files ─────────────────────────────────────────────── os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True) # Preserve existing chapters that have NO source .md file # (so we don't accidentally delete chapters built from other sources) existing = {} if os.path.exists(CHAPTERS_JSON): with open(CHAPTERS_JSON) as f: existing = json.load(f) new_nums = set(chapters_dict.keys()) merged = {**existing, **chapters_dict} # new files override existing # Re-sort by numeric key merged = dict(sorted(merged.items(), key=lambda x: int(x[0]))) with open(CHAPTERS_JSON, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(merged, f, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')) # Regenerate full search index from merged chapters full_search = [] for num_str, html_content in merged.items(): plain = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', html_content) plain = html_mod.unescape(plain) plain = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', plain).strip() full_search.append({'num': int(num_str), 'text': plain[:6000]}) full_search.sort(key=lambda x: x['num']) with open(SEARCH_JSON, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(full_search, f, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')) # ── Report ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── chap_kb = os.path.getsize(CHAPTERS_JSON) / 1024 search_kb = os.path.getsize(SEARCH_JSON) / 1024 print(f"✅ Generated from {len(files)} .md file(s)") print(f" New/updated chapters: {len(new_nums)}") if skipped: print(f" Skipped (no chapter number): {skipped}") print(f" Total chapters in index: {len(merged)}") print(f" chapters.json: {chap_kb:.1f} KB") print(f" search_index.json: {search_kb:.1f} KB") # Write outputs for subsequent steps with open(os.environ['GITHUB_OUTPUT'], 'a') as out: out.write(f"new_chapters={len(new_nums)}\n") out.write(f"total_chapters={len(merged)}\n") out.write(f"chapters_kb={chap_kb:.1f}\n") out.write(f"search_kb={search_kb:.1f}\n") PYEOF env: CHAPTERS_PATH: ${{ env.CHAPTERS_PATH }} OUTPUT_PATH: ${{ env.OUTPUT_PATH }} # ── 5. Validate output ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Validate generated files run: | CHAPTERS_FILE="${OUTPUT_PATH}/chapters.json" SEARCH_FILE="${OUTPUT_PATH}/search_index.json" for FILE in "$CHAPTERS_FILE" "$SEARCH_FILE"; do if [ ! -f "$FILE" ]; then echo "::error::Expected output file not found: $FILE" exit 1 fi # Validate JSON is parseable python3 -c "import json; data=json.load(open('${FILE}')); print(f' ✅ {\"${FILE}\".split(\"/\")[-1]}: valid JSON')" \ || { echo "::error::Invalid JSON in $FILE"; exit 1; } done CHAPTER_COUNT=$(python3 -c "import json; print(len(json.load(open('${CHAPTERS_FILE}'))))") SEARCH_COUNT=$(python3 -c "import json; print(len(json.load(open('${SEARCH_FILE}'))))") echo "chapters.json entries: ${CHAPTER_COUNT}" echo "search_index.json entries: ${SEARCH_COUNT}" if [ "$CHAPTER_COUNT" -lt 1 ]; then echo "::error::chapters.json is empty" exit 1 fi # ── 6. Commit and push ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Commit and push updated JSON files if: env.DRY_RUN != 'true' run: | git config user.name "github-actions[bot]" git config user.email "github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com" git add \ "${OUTPUT_PATH}/chapters.json" \ "${OUTPUT_PATH}/search_index.json" if git diff --cached --quiet; then echo "No changes to JSON files — skipping commit" else NEW="${{ steps.generate.outputs.new_chapters }}" TOTAL="${{ steps.generate.outputs.total_chapters }}" git commit -m "chore(data): regenerate chapters.json + search_index.json [${NEW} chapters updated, ${TOTAL} total] [skip changelog]" git push echo "Pushed updated JSON files ✅" fi # ── 7. Dry run summary ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Dry run notice if: env.DRY_RUN == 'true' run: | echo "DRY RUN — files generated but NOT committed." echo " chapters.json: ${{ steps.generate.outputs.chapters_kb }} KB" echo " search_index.json: ${{ steps.generate.outputs.search_kb }} KB" echo " Total chapters: ${{ steps.generate.outputs.total_chapters }}" # ── 8. Write job summary ───────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Write job summary if: always() run: | cat >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY << SUMMARY ## 📦 Site Data Generation | File | Size | Chapters | |---|---|---| | \`chapters.json\` | ${{ steps.generate.outputs.chapters_kb }} KB | ${{ steps.generate.outputs.total_chapters }} | | \`search_index.json\` | ${{ steps.generate.outputs.search_kb }} KB | ${{ steps.generate.outputs.total_chapters }} | - **Source path:** \`${CHAPTERS_PATH}\` - **Output path:** \`${OUTPUT_PATH}\` - **New/updated chapters this run:** ${{ steps.generate.outputs.new_chapters }} - **Dry run:** ${DRY_RUN} SUMMARY YAML echo "Created: $(wc -l < /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/generate-site-data.yml) lines"

Feature	รายละเอียด

Source convention	docs/chapters/chapter-{N}.md (N = 1–55), optional YAML frontmatter

Merge strategy	chapters ใหม่ override existing JSON, chapters อื่นที่ไม่มี source file ยังคงอยู่

Dry run	workflow_dispatch input dry_run=true → generate แต่ไม่ commit
Frontmatter fields	num, title, phase, mcp_tools, status

Codex Audit Prompt — Monolith Manufacturing OS
You are auditing the GitHub repository: indetailsgroup-hue/monolith-workspace

## TASK
Perform a full audit of the Monolith Manufacturing OS documentation system.
Check the actual files in the repo against the expected spec below.
Report PASS / WARN / FAIL for each item. Do not assume — read the actual files.

---

## 1. REPOSITORY STRUCTURE CHECK
Verify these paths exist in the repo (branch: main):

docs/
├── manufacturing-os-site/
│   ├── index.html
│   ├── style.css
│   ├── script.js
│   ├── chapters.json          ← must be valid JSON, key count >= 1
│   ├── search_index.json      ← must be valid JSON array, length >= 1
│   ├── analytics.html
│   ├── changelog.html         ← must contain <!-- COMMIT_LOG_INSERT_HERE -->
│   ├── status.html
│   └── README.md              ← must contain exactly 9 badge links
└── chapters/
    ├── chapter-01.md          ← through chapter-55.md (55 files total)
    └── ... (all 55 files)

.github/
└── workflows/
    ├── deploy-docs-pages.yml
    ├── deploy-docs-ci.yml
    ├── sync-chapters.yml
    ├── auto-update-changelog.yml
    ├── notify-slack-deploy.yml
    ├── lighthouse-audit.yml
    ├── generate-site-data.yml        ← must be v2 with caching (>= 400 lines)
    ├── auto-deploy-scispace.yml      ← NEW (>= 270 lines)
    └── validate-chapter-frontmatter.yml ← NEW (>= 300 lines)

---

## 2. WORKFLOW FILE SPEC CHECK

### generate-site-data.yml
- Has `workflow_dispatch` with `force_rebuild` input
- Has `push` trigger on `docs/chapters/**/*.md`
- Contains SHA256 caching logic referencing `.github/chapter-cache.json`
- Contains `.github/chapter-hashes-pending.json` reference
- Has at least 8 distinct steps

### auto-deploy-scispace.yml
- Has `workflow_run` trigger watching "Generate Site Data"
- Has `push` trigger on paths:
  - docs/manufacturing-os-site/chapters.json
  - docs/manufacturing-os-site/search_index.json
- Has `workflow_dispatch` with `force_deploy` input
- Has 2 jobs: `check-trigger` and `deploy`
- `deploy` job has `if: needs.check-trigger.outputs.should_deploy == 'true'`
- Uses `actions/configure-pages@v4`, `actions/upload-pages-artifact@v3`, `actions/deploy-pages@v4`
- Has Slack curl notification step
- Has `concurrency.group: "pages"`

### validate-chapter-frontmatter.yml
- Has `push` and `pull_request` triggers on `docs/chapters/**/*.md`
- Has `workflow_dispatch` with `chapters_path` and `strict_mode` inputs
- Python script validates: num, title, phase, mcp_tools, status
- Checks types: num/phase/mcp_tools must be int
- Validates status enum: complete | in-progress | planned | draft
- Cross-checks filename number vs frontmatter `num`
- Uses `::error file=...::` annotation syntax
- Has PR comment step using `actions/github-script@v7`

### lighthouse-audit.yml
- Performance threshold >= 70 (fail if below)
- Accessibility threshold >= 90 (fail if below)
- `continue-on-error: true` on threshold check step
- Uploads HTML artifact (30 days retention)
- Posts/updates PR comment with scores

---

## 3. CHAPTER MARKDOWN FRONTMATTER SPOT CHECK
Read 5 chapter files: chapter-01.md, chapter-10.md, chapter-25.md, chapter-40.md, chapter-55.md
For each verify:
- Starts with `---`
- Contains fields: num, title, phase, mcp_tools, status
- `num` is integer matching filename number
- `status` is one of: complete, in-progress, planned, draft
- `phase` is integer 1–15

---

## 4. JSON DATA FILES CHECK (if chapters.json exists)
- Is valid JSON
- Top-level is an object with string keys "1" through "55"
- Each value is a non-empty string (HTML content)
- Total keys: report actual count

For search_index.json:
- Is valid JSON array
- Each entry has `num` (int) and `text` (string) fields
- Report total entry count

---

## 5. README BADGES CHECK (docs/manufacturing-os-site/README.md)
Count total badge lines (lines starting with [![)
Expected: 9 badges
List all badge names found.

---

## 6. RECENT COMMITS SUMMARY
List the last 5 commits on main:
- Hash (short)
- Message
- Files changed count

---

## 7. WORKFLOW RUNS STATUS (last 5)
For each of these workflows, report last run status:
- generate-site-data.yml
- auto-deploy-scispace.yml
- validate-chapter-frontmatter.yml
- deploy-docs-pages.yml
- lighthouse-audit.yml

---

## OUTPUT FORMAT
For each section, output:

### [Section Name]
| Item | Expected | Actual | Status |
|------|----------|--------|--------|
| ...  | ...      | ...    | ✅ PASS / ⚠️ WARN / ❌ FAIL |

End with a summary:
## Overall Audit Summary
- Total checks: N
- Pass: N | Warn: N | Fail: N
- Critical issues (FAIL items): list them
- Recommended actions: list them

Thought
Key findings: No Cache-Control header from origin - The S3 origin does NOT send a Cache-Control header. This means CloudFront uses its default TTL which is 86400 seconds (24 hours) when no Cache-Control is set. x-amz-server-side-encryption: AES256 - Hosted on S3 server: AmazonS3 - Direct S3 origin age: 118 - This HIO52-P5 PoP cached it 118 seconds ago (from the earlier fetch) The SciSpace deploywebsite tool also doesn't expose any cache invalidation controls, which compounds the issue — without a Cache-Control header, CloudFront defaults to caching for a full 24 hours, meaning old content could persist at edge nodes until roughly 24 hours after the last modification time, unless something else is triggering invalidation. Comparing HIO52-P5 (which got new content after a Miss then Hit within 118 seconds) against BKK51-P1 (still serving old content well after that), it looks like no invalidation was actually triggered by the deploy tool, or the invalidation hasn't propagated to BKK51-P1 yet. I should be upfront that the deploy tool I have access to doesn't expose any invalidation endpoint or parameter. Since BKK51-P1 cached the old content around 04:33Z, it could persist there until roughly the same time tomorrow unless something clears it. Now I'll put together the correction report.
